### Period Lock style and drawer geometry checkpoint — 2026-09-14

- Root cause of the reported popup and overlap breakage: every Period Lock drawer is rendered with `createPortal(..., document.body)`, so the `.pc` reset contract in `src/period-closing-focus.css` (`.pc aside{position:static;inset:auto;transform:none}`) never matched. The legacy global rule `aside{position:fixed;z-index:20;inset:0 auto 0 0;width:206px}` in `src/styles.css` therefore applied the application navigation rail geometry to every drawer, pinning it to the left 206px rail in front of the sidebar and behind no scrim of its own.
The Period Locking Settings drawer is the deliberate exception to the wrapper rule: it keeps the canonical `.pc-history-drawer` shell class but not the `.pc` scope wrapper, so the page base-control rules (`.pc button`, `.pc input`, `.pc select`) cannot reach the panel. `src/period-lock-settings.css` therefore neutralises the legacy global top-bar `header` rule for its own headings with `.pls header{position:static;inset:auto;width:auto;height:auto;min-height:0;max-width:none;z-index:auto;margin:0}`. Without that reset every `.pls-card>header` inherited `position:fixed;height:58px` and the `.pls-card>header` titles stacked on top of each other at the top of the viewport. Fixed by wrapping the history and unlock-request portals (plus the six panel drawers) in `<div className="pc pc-portal-scope">` (`.pc.pc-portal-scope{display:contents}`) and giving `.pc-history-drawer` and `.pc-history-scrim` their own canonical shell in `src/period-closing.css`: `position:fixed!important`, `right:0!important`, `z-index:1400` with a `1390` scrim, `width:min(<n>px,96vw)!important` and a flex column whose `pc-drawer-body` is the only scroll container.
- Nesting and gutter flattening: `.pc-filters` lost its own border, radius and background, `.pc-register` lost its extra margin, border and shadow, `.pc-locks-panel{padding:0}` and `.pc-history-wide .pc-drawer-body .pc-panel{padding:0;border:0}`, so each level owns exactly one gutter instead of three nested bordered boxes.
- Header chrome leak fixed: `.pc-history-card>header` and `.pls-card>header` now reset `padding`, `background` and `border`, because the legacy global `header{position:fixed;z-index:10;height:58px;padding:8px 16px;background:white;border-bottom:1px solid #e5eaf1}` applied the application top bar styling to every nested card header and misaligned the settings cards inside the popup.
- Page chrome aligned to the Journal Entries heading: `.pc-heading{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) auto;column-gap:24px}` collapsing to one column at 1000px, the `All / Open / Closed` toggle wrapped in a labelled `pc-filter` so it matches the other filter controls, and the sub-900px current-period layout replaced by explicit grid placement instead of the hard-coded `margin-left:54px` offset.
- The wide lock history drawer keeps `min(820px,94vw)` between 760px and 1100px instead of collapsing to 440px, and is full width below 760px.
- No storage key, journal, close, lock, audit, enforcement or accounting rule changed. The change is styling plus three portal wrappers and one filter wrapper in `src/PeriodClosing.jsx`; `src/PeriodLockSettings.jsx` needed no markup change.
- Validation run in this session: `tests/period-closing-ui.test.mjs` **16 passed, 0 failed** (two new regression contracts pin the drawer shell geometry and the page chrome), `tests/period-lock-settings-ui.test.mjs` **13/13**, `tests/period-locking.test.mjs` **24/24**, `tests/period-scope-visibility.test.mjs` **16/16**, `tests/period-unlock-scope.test.mjs` **9/9** and `tests/journal-create-ui.test.mjs` **22/22**.
- Full suite: **306 passed, 11 failed across 57 runnable suites of 61 test files**. All 11 failures are the same pre-existing, unrelated stale assertions listed above, and the four `esbuild` suites still cannot start (`spawn EPERM`).
- Structural check: the sandbox SSR harness renders all 13 Period Lock surfaces and a tag-stack probe over that markup reports **STRUCTURE OK - 13 surfaces well formed** (no nested buttons, labels or forms, and no overlapping close tags). `tmp/pc2-ssr.mjs` renders 315,499 characters and `tmp/pc2-drawers.mjs` all 12 drawers.
- Served check: `http://localhost:5173/` returned HTTP 200 for `src/period-closing.css`, `src/period-closing-focus.css`, `src/period-lock-settings.css` and `src/period-locking.js`.
- Not verified: no rendered visual pass. In-app browser automation is rejected by the environment auto-review, and no production build was possible because `vite build` needs the esbuild binary this sandbox cannot spawn.

### Purchase accounting integration checkpoint — 2026-09-07

- Added non-posting Goods Receipts with cumulative quantity validation, partial receipts, and Purchase Order traceability.
- Purchase Orders can produce multiple bills for received-but-unbilled quantities; over-receipt and overbilling are rejected.
